for each
n
by induction.
Recall from the proof of Lemma
13.15
that

\mu_{n}=RJ\left(\varepsilon_{J^{n-1}LX}\circ L\left(\mu_{n-1}\right)\right).

We will take
\tilde{A}_{1}
to be induced in the obvious way from the first commuting square in the diagram. Suppose the lift
\tilde{A}_{n}
has been defined. By naturality and inductive hypothesis, the following diagram commutes up to canonical homotopy

\textstyle{L\left(\left(RJL\right)^{n-1}X\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{L\left(\left(RJL\right)^{n-1}\left(\tilde{\beta}\right)\right)}
\scriptstyle{L\left(\mu_{n-1}\right)}
\textstyle{L\left(\left(RJL\right)^{n}X\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{L\left(\mu_{n}\right)}
\textstyle{L\left(RJ^{n-1}LX\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{L\left(RJ^{n-1}\left(\beta\right)\right)}
\scriptstyle{\varepsilon_{J^{n-1}LX}}
\textstyle{L\left(RJ^{n}LX\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\varepsilon_{J^{n}LX}}
\textstyle{J^{n-1}LX\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{J^{n-1}\left(\beta\right)}
\textstyle{J^{n}LX}

The image of this diagram under
RJ
yields a commuting diagram

\textstyle{\left(RJL\right)^{n}X\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\left(\left(RJL\right)^{n}\left(\tilde{\beta}\right)\right)}
\scriptstyle{RJL\left(\mu_{n-1}\right)}
\textstyle{\left(\left(RJL\right)^{n+1}X\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{RJL\left(\mu_{n}\right)}
\textstyle{\left(RJLJ^{n-1}LX\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\left(RJ^{n}\left(\beta\right)\right)}
\scriptstyle{RJ\left(\varepsilon_{J^{n-1}LX}\right)}
\textstyle{\left(RJLJ^{n}LX\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{R\left(\varepsilon_{J^{n}LX}\right)}
\textstyle{RJ^{n}LX\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{RJ^{n}\left(\beta\right)}
\textstyle{RJ^{n+1}LX.}

Taking the outer square induces a lift
\tilde{A}_{n+1}.
Thus, there exists a lift
A
as claimed. Notice that the
n^{th}
dotted arrow is
\left(RJL\right)^{n-1}\left(\tilde{\beta}\right).
It follows that the lift
A
exhibits

\tilde{\beta}:X\to RJLX

as an
RJL
-coalgebra. Notice that for any
\infty
-category
\mathcal{K},
the same logic can be applied to construct in the same inductive fashion an
RJL_{*}
-coalgebra in
\operatorname{Fun}\left(\mathcal{K},\mathscr{C}\right)
on
R\ \circ G
for any
J_{*}
-coalgebra
G
in
\operatorname{Fun}\left(\mathcal{K},\mathscr{D}\right)
for which
G=L\circ F,
for some

F:\mathcal{K}\to\mathscr{C}.

Moreover, this can be done in such a way that it agrees with the induced
RJL
-coalgebra structure on
RLF\left(x\right)
from the
J
-coalgebra structure on
LF\left(x\right),
for every object
x
of
\mathcal{K}.
Applying this to
\mathcal{K}=\Delta^{n}
implies that the construction induces a functor of
\infty
-categories

\kappa:\mathscr{C}\times_{\mathscr{D}}\mathbf{CoAlg}_{J}\left(\mathscr{D}\right)\to\mathbf{CoAlg}_{RJL}\left(\mathscr{C}\right),

where

\textstyle{\mathscr{C}\times_{\mathscr{D}}\mathbf{CoAlg}_{J}\left(\mathscr{D}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{U^{\mathscr{C}}_{J}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{CoAlg}_{J}\left(\mathscr{D}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{U_{J}}
\textstyle{\mathscr{C}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{L}
\textstyle{\mathscr{D}}

pullback diagram of
\infty
-categories.
Moreover, the functor

\tilde{\rho}:\mathbf{CoAlg}_{RJL}\left(\mathscr{C}\right)\to\mathbf{CoAlg}_{J}\left(\mathscr{D}\right)

induces a functor

\widehat{\rho}:\mathbf{CoAlg}_{RJL}\left(\mathscr{C}\right)\to\mathscr{C}\times_{\mathscr{D}}\mathbf{CoAlg}_{J}\left(\mathscr{D}\right).

It is easy to check that these two functors are inverse to one another.
∎

14.
Linear differential operators and D-modules

Definition 14.1
.

Let
\mathcal{X}\in\mathcal{E}_{\mathfrak{Der}}
and consider the morphism
\eta:\mathcal{X}\to\mathcal{X}_{dR}
to its de Rham stack and the induced adjunction between the
\infty
-categories of modules:

\textstyle{\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\eta_{\star}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}_{dR}}}.\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\eta^{\star}}

Denote the comonad

\eta^{\star}\eta_{\star}:\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}}\to\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}}

by
\mathcal{J}^{\infty}.
For
\mathcal{F}
an
\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}
-module,
\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{F}
is called the
jet module
of
\mathcal{F}.

Definition 14.2
.

Let
\mathcal{X}
be a derived stack and let
\mathcal{F}
and
\mathcal{G}
be
\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}
-modules A
linear differential operator
from
\mathcal{F}
to
\mathcal{G}
is a morphism
\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{F}\to\mathcal{G}
. We will call the
\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}
-module

\mathsf{Diff}\left(\mathcal{F},\mathcal{G}\right):=\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{X}}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{F},\mathcal{G}\right)

the
module of linear differential operators
from
\mathcal{F}
to
\mathcal{G}.
By the
sheaf of differential operators on
\mathcal{X}
we mean the
\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}
-module

\mathsf{Diff}\left(\mathcal{X}\right):=\mathsf{Diff}\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}\right).

Lemma 14.3
.

With
\mathcal{F}
and
\mathcal{G}
as above, there is a canonical equivalence of
\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}_{dR}}
-modules

\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}_{dR}}}\left(\eta_{\star}\mathcal{F},\eta_{\star}\mathcal{G}\right)\simeq\eta_{\star}\mathsf{Diff}\left(\mathcal{F},\mathcal{G}\right).

Proof.

Let
\mathcal{H}
be any
\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}_{dR}}
-module. Then we have the following string of natural equivalences

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}}\left(\mathcal{H},\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}_{dR}}}\left(\eta_{\star}\mathcal{F},\eta_{\star}\mathcal{G}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}_{dR}}}\left(\mathcal{H}\underset{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}_{dR}}}{\otimes}\eta_{\star}\mathcal{F},\eta_{\star}\mathcal{G}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}}\left(\eta^{\star}\left(\mathcal{H}\underset{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}_{dR}}}{\otimes}\eta_{\star}\mathcal{F}\right),\mathcal{G}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}}\left(\eta^{\star}\mathcal{H}\underset{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}}{\otimes}\eta^{\star}\eta_{\star}\mathcal{F},\mathcal{G}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}}\left(\eta^{\star}\mathcal{H},\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{F},\mathcal{G}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}_{dR}}}\left(\mathcal{H},\eta_{\star}\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{F},\mathcal{G}\right)\right).

∎

Remark 14.4
.

Notice that
\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}_{dR}}}\left(\eta_{\star}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}},\eta_{\star}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}\right)
is the internal endomorphism algebra of
\eta_{\star}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}},
hence has the structure of an associative algebra object. In particular, it has the structure of a sheaf on
\mathcal{E}_{\mathfrak{Der}}/\mathcal{X}_{dR}
with values in associative
\mathbb{R}
-algebras.

Proposition 14.5
.

Let
u:U\to\mathcal{X}
be formally étale. Then
\mathsf{Diff}\left(\mathcal{X}\right)\left(U\right)
has the canonical structure of an associative
\mathbb{R}
-algebra.

Proof.

Since
u
is formally étale, we have a canonical identification
U\simeq\eta^{*}\left(U_{dR}\right).
Hence

\displaystyle\mathsf{Diff}\left(\mathcal{X}\right)\left(U\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathsf{Diff}\left(\mathcal{X}\right)\left(\eta^{*}\left(U_{dR}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\left(\eta_{\star}\mathsf{Diff}\left(\mathcal{X}\right)\right)\left(U_{dR}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}_{dR}}}\left(\eta_{\star}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}},\eta_{\star}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}\right)\left(U_{dR}\right).

The result now follows form Remark
14.4
.
∎

Corollary 14.6
.

For
\mathcal{X}
any
{\mathbf{SC}^{\infty}}
-Deligne Mumford stack, for example a derived orbifold, then
\mathsf{Diff}\left(\mathcal{X}\right)
restricts to a sheaf
\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{X}}
of associative
\mathbb{R}
-algebras on the small site of
\mathcal{X}.

Remark 14.7
.

For
\mathcal{X}
any
{\mathbf{SC}^{\infty}}
-Deligne Mumford stack, since
\mathsf{Diff}\left(\mathcal{X}\right)
is a sheaf of
\mathcal{O}^{\mathcal{E}_{\mathfrak{Der}}}_{\mathcal{X}}
-modules (on the large site), its restriction to the small site is a sheaf of
\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{X}}
-modules, hence a quasicoherent sheaf by Lemma
9.57
.

Definition 14.8
.

Let
\mathcal{X}
any
{\mathbf{SC}^{\infty}}
-Deligne Mumford stack. The
\infty
-category
\mathcal{D}\mbox{-}\mathbf{Mod}\left(\mathcal{X}\right)
of (left)
\mathcal{D}
-modules over
\mathcal{X},
is the
\infty
-category of left modules of
\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{X}}
in
\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{X}\right).

Let
\left(\tilde{\mathcal{X}},\mathcal{O}_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\right)
be a
{\mathbf{SC}^{\infty}}
-Deligne Mumford stack with functor of points
\mathcal{X}
. Notice that there is a canonical map of sheaves of
\mathbb{R}
-algebras

b:\mathcal{O}_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}\hookrightarrow\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{X}}

over
\tilde{\mathcal{X}}
which hence induces an adjunction

\textstyle{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{X}\right)=\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\tilde{\mathcal{X}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{b_{!}}
\textstyle{\mathcal{D}\mbox{-}\mathbf{Mod}\left(\mathcal{X}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces,}
\scriptstyle{b^{*}}

with
b_{!}\dashv b^{*},
where
b^{*}
is the restriction of scalars functor, and where
b_{!}
is the induced module functor. Thus,
b^{*}b_{!}:=\mbox{\textcyrup{D}}_{\mathcal{X}}
is a monad on
\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{X}\right).
Explicitly, it is given by

\mbox{\textcyrup{D}}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathcal{F}\right)=\mathscr{D}_{\mathcal{X}}\underset{\mathcal{O}_{\tilde{\mathcal{M}}}}{\otimes}\mathcal{F}.

Proposition 14.9
.

Let
M
be a smooth manifold. Then
\mathcal{D}_{M}
is the sheaf which assigns each open subset
U
of
M
the ring of linear differential operators of
\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(U\right)
which are
locally
of finite order.

Proof.

By Remark
14.7
,
\mathcal{D}_{M}
is quasicoherent. Since for each
U\subseteq M
,
U
is also a manifold and
\mathcal{D}_{U}=\mathcal{D}_{M}|_{U},
it suffices to prove the statement at the level of global sections. Global sections of
\mathcal{D}_{M}
is the same as global sections of
\mathsf{Diff}\left(M\right)
on the large site. Since
\mathcal{D}_{M}
and
\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{M}
are concentrated in degree zero, this is the same as the subset of maps

\textstyle{\mathcal{J}^{\infty}_{M}\left(M\times\mathbb{R}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{M\times\mathbb{R}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{M,}

i.e. elements of
\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathcal{E}_{\mathfrak{Der}}/M}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}_{M}\left(M\times\mathbb{R}\right),M\times\mathbb{R}\right),
which respect the
\mathcal{O}_{M}
-module structure. By Corollaries
12.18
and
6.38
, this is the same as morphisms of Frechét manifolds over
M,
which are fiberwise linear, i.e. morphisms of Frechét vector bundles from
\mathcal{J}^{\infty}_{M}\left(M\times\mathbb{R}\right)
to
\mathbb{R}\times M.
These can be identified with the linear differential operators that are locally of finite order, i.e. a locally finite sum

\sum\limits_{k=0}^{\infty}f_{k}D_{k},

with each
D_{k}
a linear operator of order
k.

∎

Corollary 14.10
.

Let
M
be a manifold, then there is an equivalence of quasicoherent sheaves

\mathcal{D}_{M}\simeq\underset{k}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\mathcal{O}_{M}\right)^{\vee},

where
\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\mathcal{O}_{M}\right)^{\vee}
is dual to the module of sections of the
k^{th}
order jet bundle (of the trivial
\mathbb{R}
-bundle).

Proof.

The description of
\mathcal{D}_{M}
from Proposition
14.9
identifies
\mathcal{D}_{M}
with global sections of the sheafification of the pointwise filtered colimit of the modules of finite order linear differential operators. Since the dual space to
\mathcal{J}^{k}\mathcal{O}_{M}
is the space of linear differential operators of degree
k,
this completes the proof.
∎

Proposition 14.11
.

Suppose that
\mathcal{M}
is a supermanifold. Then

\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{M}}\simeq\underset{k}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{\vee}.

Proof.

As this is a statement about sheaves, it suffices to check it locally, so it suffices to prove it for
\mathcal{M}=\mathbb{R}^{p|q}.
By Lemma
12.17
, it follows that there is a canonical equivalence

\Gamma\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\left(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)\right)\cong\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\times\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)_{(k)}\right).

Since for
k\geq q,

\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)_{(k)}\simeq\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)_{\left(k\right)}\times\mathbb{R}^{0|q},
it follows that

\Gamma\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\left(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)\right)\cong\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)_{\left(k\right)}\right)\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right].

Thus

\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\simeq\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}_{\left(k\right)}\right)\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right)\right].

Note that
\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}_{\left(k\right)}\right)
is a finite dimensional vector space and
\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)
is a finite dimensional super vector space, and we are inducing these
\mathbb{R}
-module to
\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right).
It follows that we can rewrite this as

\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\simeq\left(\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}_{\left(k\right)}\right)\right]\right)\otimes\left[\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right].

Note that

\displaystyle\Gamma\left(\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}_{\left(k\right)}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}_{\left(k\right)}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}_{\left(k\right)}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\left(\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}_{\left(k\right)}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)}{\otimes}\left(\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}_{\left(k\right)}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)}{\otimes}\Gamma\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right).

So if
pr:\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\to\mathbb{R}^{p}
denotes the canonical projection, we have

\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}_{\left(k\right)}\right)\right]\simeq pr^{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}},

and thus

\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\simeq pr^{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)\otimes\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right).

Since
\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)
is a finite dimensional super vector space, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)}\left(pr^{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)\otimes\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right),\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)}\left(pr^{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right),\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)}\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right),\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)}\left(pr^{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right),\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right)^{\vee}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)}\left(pr^{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right),\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\right)\otimes\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right)^{\vee}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\left(pr^{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)\right)^{\vee}\otimes\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right)^{\vee}.

Notice that the underlying topological space of
\mathbb{R}^{p|q}
is
\mathbb{R}^{p},
so
pr_{\star}
is simply the restriction of scalars for the morphism of sheaves of
{\mathbf{SC}^{\infty}}
-rings

pr:\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\to\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}

on
\mathbb{R}^{p}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\Gamma\left[\left(pr^{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)\right)^{\vee}\right]

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)}\left(pr^{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right),\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}},pr_{\star}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}},\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\Gamma\left(\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)^{\vee}\right)\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}\right)}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p|q}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\Gamma\left(\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)^{\vee}\underset{\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}}{\otimes}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\Gamma\left(pr^{\star}\left(\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)^{\vee}\right)\right),

and hence

\left(pr^{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)\right)^{\vee}\simeq\left(pr^{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)^{\vee}\right).

Thus we have that

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\left(pr^{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)^{\vee}\right)\otimes\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right)^{\vee}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle pr^{\star}\left(\underset{k}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)^{\vee}\right)\otimes\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right)^{\vee}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left[\left(pr^{\star}\left(\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)^{\vee}\right)\right)\otimes\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right)^{\vee}\right]

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left[\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}}{\otimes}\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p}}\right)^{\vee}\right)\otimes\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right)^{\vee}\right]

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left[\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}_{\left(k\right)}\right)^{\vee}\right)\otimes\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right)^{\vee}\right]

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left[\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\left(\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{p}_{\left(k\right)}\right)\otimes\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\underset{\mathbb{R}}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^{0|q}\right)\right)\right)^{\vee}\right]

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left[\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{R}^{p|q}}\right)^{\vee}\right].

∎

Proposition 14.12
.

Let
\mathcal{M}
be a supermanifold. Then the functor
\Lambda_{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda^{\star}
is right adjoint to
\mbox{\textcyrup{D}}_{\mathcal{M}}.

Proof.

Let
\mathcal{F}
be an arbitrary quasicoherent sheaf on
\mathcal{M}.
Since
\Lambda_{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}
is also quasicoherent, it is determined by its global sections. These are the same as global sections of
\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}.
By Proposition
12.11
and Lemma
12.12
it follows that

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\eta^{\star}\eta_{\star}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\right)\left(\mathcal{M}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\left(\eta^{*}\eta_{!}\left(id_{M}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\left(T^{\infty}\mathcal{M}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\left(\underset{k}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\mathcal{M}_{\left(k\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\left(\mathcal{M}_{\left(k\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}_{\left(k\right)}\right)\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}{\widehat{\otimes}}\mathcal{F}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\Gamma_{M}\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}_{M}\left(M\times\mathbb{R}\right)\right)\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}{\widehat{\otimes}}\mathcal{F}\left(\mathcal{M}\right).

Thus, we deduce that there is an equivalence of quasicoherent sheaves:

\Lambda_{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\simeq\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathcal{J}^{k}\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)\otimes\mathcal{F}\right].

Since
\mathcal{J}^{k}\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)
is locally free of finite rank (it is a finite dimensional vector bundle), we have

\Lambda_{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\simeq\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)\right)^{\vee},\mathcal{F}\right).

Let
\mathcal{G}
be any quasicoherent sheaf. Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\mathcal{G},\Lambda_{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\mathcal{G},\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)\right)^{\vee},\mathcal{F}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\ \mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\mathcal{G},\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)\right)^{\vee},\mathcal{F}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)\right)^{\vee}\otimes\mathcal{G},\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\underset{k}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left[\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)\right)^{\vee}\otimes\mathcal{G}\right],\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\underset{k}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left[\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\left(\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)\right)^{\vee}\right]\otimes\mathcal{G},\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{M}}\otimes\mathcal{G},\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\mbox{\textcyrup{D}}_{\mathcal{M}}\left(\mathcal{G}\right),\mathcal{F}\right).

∎

Remark 14.13
.

Recall that there is a canonical homomorphism of sheaves

b:\mathcal{O}_{\tilde{\mathcal{M}}}\hookrightarrow\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{M}}

and we have

\textstyle{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)=\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\tilde{\mathcal{M}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{b_{!}}
\textstyle{\mathcal{D}\mbox{-}\mathbf{Mod}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces.}
\scriptstyle{b^{*}}

So
b_{!}\dashv b^{*}.
There is a further adjoint
b_{*}
such that

b_{!}\dashv b^{*}\dashv b_{*},

where

b_{*}:\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\to\mathcal{D}\mbox{-}\mathbf{Mod}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)

sends a quasicoherent sheaf
\mathcal{F}
to its
coinduced
\mathcal{D}
-module
. The underlying sheaf of vector spaces of
b_{*}\mathcal{F}
is the same as

\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}}\left(\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{M}},\mathcal{F}\right),
but the
\mathcal{D}
-module structure is defined by

\left(D\cdot\varphi\right)\left(x\right)=\varphi\left(x\cdot D\right).

Notice that

\mbox{\textcyrup{D}}_{\mathcal{X}}=b^{*}b_{!}\dashv b^{*}b_{*},

so by Proposition
14.12
and the uniqueness of adjoints, we must have that

\Lambda_{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda^{\star}\simeq b^{*}b_{*}.

Notice that
b^{*}b_{*}
has a canonical comonad structure.

Lemma 14.14
.

Let
\mathcal{M}
be a supermanifold and let
\mathcal{F}
be quasi-coherent sheaf over
\mathcal{F}.
Denote by
\varepsilon
the counit of the adjunction

\Lambda^{\star}\dashv\Lambda_{\star}.

Then for all
n\geq 1,

\Lambda_{\star}\left(\varepsilon_{\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\right)^{n}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}}\right):\left(\Lambda_{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda^{\star}\right)^{n}\left(\mathcal{F}\right)\to\Lambda_{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\right)^{n}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}

is an equivalence.

Similarly to the proof of Proposition
14.12
, if
\mathcal{F}
is an arbitrary quasicoherent sheaf on
\mathcal{M},

\displaystyle\Gamma\Lambda_{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\right)^{n}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\left(\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\right)\left(\left(\eta_{!}\eta^{*}\right)^{n}\left(id_{\mathcal{M}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\left(\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\right)\left(\left(T_{\mathcal{M}}^{\infty}\right)^{n}\left(id_{\mathcal{M}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\left(\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\right)\left(T^{\infty}\mathcal{M}\times_{\mathcal{M}}T^{\infty}\mathcal{M}\times_{\mathcal{M}}\ldots\times_{\mathcal{M}}T^{\infty}\mathcal{M}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\left(\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\right)\left(\underset{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left[\mathcal{M}_{\left(k_{1}\right)}\times_{\mathcal{M}}\mathcal{M}_{\left(k_{2}\right)}\times_{\mathcal{M}}\ldots\times_{\mathcal{M}}\mathcal{M}_{\left(k_{n}\right)}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left(\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\right)\left(\left[\mathcal{M}_{\left(k_{1}\right)}\times_{\mathcal{M}}\mathcal{M}_{\left(k_{2}\right)}\times_{\mathcal{M}}\ldots\times_{\mathcal{M}}\mathcal{M}_{\left(k_{n}\right)}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}_{\left(k_{1}\right)}\times_{\mathcal{M}}\mathcal{M}_{\left(k_{2}\right)}\times_{\mathcal{M}}\ldots\times_{\mathcal{M}}\mathcal{M}_{\left(k_{n}\right)}\right)\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}{\otimes}\mathcal{F}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\right]

Notice that each
\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)_{\left(k\right)}
is a split nilpotent extension over
\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}\right),
hence the
\mathbf{C}^{\infty}
-tensor product agrees with that of commutative
\mathbb{R}
-algebras, and we deduce

\displaystyle\Gamma\Lambda_{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\right)^{n}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}_{\left(k_{1}\right)}\right)\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}{\otimes}\ldots\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}{\otimes}\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}_{\left(k_{n}\right)}\right)\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}{\otimes}\mathcal{F}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\Gamma\left(\mathcal{J}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}{\otimes}\ldots\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}{\otimes}\Gamma\left(\mathcal{J}^{k_{n}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)\underset{\mathbf{C}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}{\otimes}\mathcal{F}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\right]

Hence

\Lambda_{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\right)^{n}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\simeq\underset{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathcal{J}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\otimes\ldots\mathcal{J}^{k_{n}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\otimes\mathcal{F}\right].

So, for any quasicoherent sheaf
\mathcal{F},
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\mathcal{G},\Lambda_{\star}\left(\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\right)^{n}\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\mathcal{G},\underset{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left[\mathcal{J}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathcal{J}^{k_{n}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\otimes\mathcal{F}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\mathcal{G},\mathcal{J}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathcal{J}^{k_{n}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\otimes\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\mathcal{G},\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\left(\mathcal{J}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{\vee}\otimes\ldots\otimes\left(\mathcal{J}^{k_{n}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{\vee},\mathcal{F}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\underset{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\left(\mathcal{J}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{\vee}\otimes\ldots\otimes\left(\mathcal{J}^{k_{n}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{\vee}\otimes\mathcal{G},\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\underset{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left[\left(\mathcal{J}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{\vee}\otimes\ldots\otimes\left(\mathcal{J}^{k_{n}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{\vee}\otimes\mathcal{G}\right],\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\left[\underset{k_{1}}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left(\mathcal{J}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{\vee}\right]\otimes\ldots\otimes\left[\underset{k_{n}}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left(\mathcal{J}^{k_{n}}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{\vee}\right]\otimes\mathcal{G},\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{M}}\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{M}}\otimes\mathcal{G},\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\mbox{\textcyrup{D}}^{n}\left(\mathcal{G}\right),\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}\mathrm{ap}_{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)}\left(\mathcal{G},\left(\Lambda_{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda^{\star}\right)^{n}\left(\mathcal{F}\right)\right).

Theorem 14.15
.

Let
\mathcal{M}
be a supermanifold. Then there is a canonical equivalence

\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}_{dR}\right)\simeq\mathcal{D}\mbox{-}\mathbf{Mod}\left(\mathcal{M}\right).

Proof.

Since
\mathcal{M}
is formally smooth (Corollary
10.32
), the adjunction

\eta^{\star}\dashv\eta_{\star}

is comonadic by Corollary
13.10
. Hence there is a canonical equivalence

\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}_{dR}}}\simeq\mathbf{CoAlg}_{\mathcal{J}^{\infty}}\left(\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}}\right).

Consider the adjunction

\textstyle{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\Lambda^{\star}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\Lambda_{\star}}

with
\Lambda^{\star}\dashv\Lambda_{\star},
with
\Lambda^{\star}
the fully faithful inclusion. Recall that by Corollary
13.12
that there is a pullback diagram

\textstyle{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}_{dR}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\Lambda_{dR}^{\star}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}_{dR}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\eta^{\star}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\Lambda^{\star}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}}.}

Putting these two facts together implies that there is a pullback diagram

\textstyle{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}_{dR}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\Lambda_{dR}^{\star}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{CoAlg}_{\mathcal{J}^{\infty}}\left(\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{U_{\mathcal{J}^{\infty}}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\Lambda^{\star}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}}.}

By Lemma
14.14
, the adjunction
\Lambda^{\star}\dashv\Lambda_{\star}
and the comonad
\mathcal{J}^{\infty}
satisfy the hypothesis of Theorem
13.20
. It follows that
\mathcal{J}^{\infty}
induces comonad structure on
\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda_{\star}
— which by direct inspection agrees with the one coming from the identification

\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda_{\star}\simeq b^{*}b_{*}

of Remark
14.13
— and a pullback diagram

\textstyle{\mathbf{CoAlg}_{\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda_{\star}}\left(\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{U_{\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda_{\star}}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{CoAlg}_{\mathcal{J}^{\infty}}\left(\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{U_{\mathcal{J}^{\infty}}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\Lambda^{\star}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}}.}

Hence, we must have

\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}_{dR}\right)\simeq\mathbf{CoAlg}_{\Lambda^{\star}\mathcal{J}^{\infty}\Lambda_{\star}}\left(\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\right)\simeq\mathbf{CoAlg}_{b_{*}b^{*}}\left(\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\right).

Notice that there is a triple of adjunctions

\textstyle{\mathcal{D}\mbox{-}\mathbf{Mod}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{b^{\star}}
\textstyle{\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right).\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{b_{!}}
\scriptstyle{b_{\ast}}

The functor
b^{*}
is both a left and right adjoint and clearly is conservative, hence

b_{!}\dashv b^{*}

is monadic and

b^{*}\dashv b_{*}

is comonadic. In particular,

\mathbf{CoAlg}_{b_{*}b^{*}}\left(\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\right)\simeq\mathcal{D}\mbox{-}\mathbf{Mod}\left(\mathcal{M}\right).

This completes the proof.
∎

Remark 14.16
.

We can draw several observations from the proof of Theorem
14.15
. Firstly, under the equivalence

\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}_{dR}\right)\simeq\mathcal{D}\mbox{-}\mathbf{Mod}\left(\mathcal{M}\right),

we have an identification of

b^{*}:\mathcal{D}\mbox{-}\mathbf{Mod}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)\to\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right)

with

\eta_{\mathbf{QC}}^{\star}:\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}_{dR}\right)\to\mathbf{QC}\left(\mathcal{M}\right),

and by uniqueness of adjoints, an identification of
\eta^{\mathbf{QC}}_{\star}
with
b_{*},
where
b_{*}
is the functor sending an
\tilde{\mathcal{O}}_{\mathcal{M}}
-module to its
co
-induced (left)
\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{M}}
-module. This means as a
\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{M}}
-module, we have

\eta^{\mathbf{QC}}_{\star}\mathcal{F}=\mathbf{Mod}_{\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}}\left(\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{M}},\mathcal{F}\right),

with the obvious
\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{M}}
-module structure. This is commonly called the
jet module
of
\mathcal{F}
. Notice that since
\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{M}}\simeq\underset{k}{\underrightarrow{\mbox{colim}\vskip 0.5pt}\mspace{4mu}}\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{\vee},
we have the equivalent description
\underset{k}{\varprojlim\mspace{3mu}}\left(\mathcal{J}^{k}\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{M}}\otimes\mathcal{F}\right).

Moreover, since
b^{*}
admits a left adjoint
b_{!}
, the
induced module functor
,
\eta_{\mathbf{QC}}^{\star}
also admits a left adjoint
\eta_{!}^{\mathbf{QC}},
which can be identified with
b_{!}
under the above equivalence. Moreover, the adjunction

\eta_{!}^{\mathbf{QC}}\dashv\eta^{\star}_{\mathbf{QC}}

is monadic and there is a canonical identification of monads

\eta^{\star}_{\mathbf{QC}}\circ\eta_{!}^{\mathbf{QC}}\simeq\mbox{\textcyrup{D}}_{\mathcal{X}}\simeq\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{M}}\underset{\tilde{\mathcal{O}}_{\mathcal{M}}}{\otimes}\left(\mspace{3mu}\cdot\mspace{3mu}\right)
